translate name, supers and subs to unicode characters\n        def translate(s):\n            if s in greek_unicode:\n                return greek_unicode.get(s)\n            else:\n                return s\n\n        name, supers, subs = split_super_sub(sym.name)\n        name = translate(name)\n        supers = [translate(sup) for sup in supers]\n        subs = [translate(sub) for sub in subs]\n\n        mname = self.dom.createElement('mml:mi')\n        mname.appendChild(self.dom.createTextNode(name))\n        if len(supers) == 0:\n            if len(subs) == 0:\n                ci.appendChild(self.dom.createTextNode(name))\n            else:\n                msub = self.dom.createElement('mml:msub')\n                msub.appendChild(mname)\n                msub.appendChild(join(subs))\n                ci.appendChild(msub)\n        else:\n            if len(subs) == 0:\n                msup = self.dom.createElement('mml:msup')\n                msup.appendChild(mname)\n                msup.appendChild(join(supers))\n                ci.appendChild(msup)\n            else:\n                msubsup = self.dom.createElement('mml:msubsup')\n                msubsup.appendChild(mname)\n                msubsup.appendChild(join(subs))\n                msubsup.appendChild(join(supers))\n                ci.appendChild(msubsup)\n        return ci\n\n    _print_MatrixSymbol = _print_Symbol\n    _print_RandomSymbol = _print_Symbol\n\n    def _print_Pow(self, e):\n        # Here we use root instead of power if the exponent is the reciprocal of an integer\n        if self._settings['root_notation'] and e.exp.is_Rational and e.exp.p == 1:\n            x = self.dom.createElement('apply')\n            x.appendChild(self.dom.createElement('root'))\n            if e.exp.q != 2:\n                xmldeg = self.dom.createElement('degree')\n                xmlci = self.dom.createElement('ci')\n                xmlci.appendChild(self.dom.createTextNode(str(e.exp.q)))\n                xmldeg.appendChild(xmlci)\n                x.appendChild(xmldeg)\n            x.appendChild(self._print(e.base))\n            return x\n\n        x = self.dom.createElement('apply')\n        x_1 = self.dom.createElement(self.mathml_tag(e))\n        x.appendChild(x_1)\n        x.appendChild(self._print(e.base))\n        x.appendChild(self._print(e.exp))\n        return x\n\n    def _print_Number(self, e):\n        x = self.dom.createElement(self.mathml_tag(e))\n        x.appendChild(self.dom.createTextNode(str(e)))\n        return x\n\n\n[551 more lines in file. The three return values are the actual name, a\n    list with superscripts and a list with subscripts.\n\n    Examples\n    ========\n\n    >>> from sympy.printing.conventions import split_super_sub\n    >>> split_super_sub('a_x^1')\n    ('a', ['1'], ['x'])\n    >>> split_super_sub('var_sub1__sup_sub2')\n    ('var', ['sup'], ['sub1', 'sub2'])\n\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(text) == 0:\n        return text, [], []\n\n    pos = 0\n    name = None\n    supers = []\n    subs = []\n    while pos < len(text):\n        start = pos + 1\n        if text[pos:pos + 2] == \"__\":\n            start += 1\n        pos_hat = text.find(\"^\", start)\n        if pos_hat < 0:\n            pos_hat = len(text)\n        pos_usc = text.find(\"_\", start)\n        if pos_usc < 0:\n            pos_usc = len(text)\n        pos_next = min(pos_hat, pos_usc)\n        part = text[pos:pos_next]\n        pos = pos_next\n        if name is None:\n            name = part\n        elif part.startswith(\"^\"):\n            supers.append(part[1:])\n        elif part.startswith(\"__\"):\n            supers.append(part[2:])\n        elif part.startswith(\"_\"):\n            subs.append(part[1:])\n        else:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"This should never happen.\")\n\n    # make a little exception when a name ends with digits, i.e. treat them\n    # as a subscript too.\n    m = _name_with_digits_p.match(name)\n    if m:\n        name, sub = m.groups()\n        subs.insert(0, sub)\n\n    return name, supers, subs\n\n\ndef requires_partial(expr):\n    \"\"\"Return whether a partial derivative symbol is required for printing\n\n    This requires checking how many free variables there are,\n    filtering out the ones that are integers. Some expressions don't have\n    free variables. In that case, check its variable list explicitly to\n    get the context of the expression.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    if not isinstance(expr.free_symbols, Iterable):\n        return len(set(expr.variables)) > 1\n\n    return sum(not s.is_integer for s in expr.free_symbols) > 1\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1786648208223}]}

Per the MathML spec, `<mi>` is a token element that may only contain text. Browsers (Safari 12.0.2) don't render script elements nested inside `<mi>`, so `x2` disappeared entirely while plain `x` rendered fine.\n\n**Fix** (`sympy/printing/mathml.py`): The root element is now built directly:\n- No scripts → root is the `<mi>` element itself (`<mi>x</mi>`)\n- Subscript → root is `<msub>`\n- Superscript → root is `<msup>`\n- Both → root is `<msubsup>`\n\nThe `mathvariant=\"bold\"` attribute (for bold matrix symbols) is applied to whichever element is the root. This matches the fix that shipped in SymPy 1.4.\n\n**Result:** `x2` now prints as `<msub><mi>x</mi><mi>2</mi></msub>` — valid MathML that renders correctly.\n\n**Tests** (`sympy/printing/tests/test_mathml.py`): Updated `test_presentation_symbol` assertions to expect the script element as root. Each superscript is\n    preceded with a \"^\" character or by \"__\". Each subscript is preceded\n    by a \"_\" character.  The three return values are the actual name, a\n    list with superscripts and a list with subscripts.\n\n    Examples\n    ========\n\n    >>> from sympy.printing.conventions import split_super_sub\n    >>> split_super_sub('a_x^1')\n    ('a', ['1'], ['x'])\n    >>> split_super_sub('var_sub1__sup_sub2')\n    ('var', ['sup'], ['sub1', 'sub2'])\n\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(text) == 0:\n        return text, [], []\n\n    pos = 0\n    name = None\n    supers = []\n    subs = []\n    while pos < len(text):\n        start = pos + 1\n        if text[pos:pos + 2] == \"__\":\n            start += 1\n        pos_hat = text.find(\"^\", start)\n        if pos_hat < 0:\n            pos_hat = len(text)\n        pos_usc = text.find(\"_\", start)\n        if pos_usc < 0:\n            pos_usc = len(text)\n        pos_next = min(pos_hat, pos_usc)\n        part = text[pos:pos_next]\n        pos = pos_next\n        if name is None:\n            name = part\n        elif part.startswith(\"^\"):\n            supers.append(part[1:])\n        elif part.startswith(\"__\"):\n            supers.append(part[2:])\n        elif part.startswith(\"_\"):\n            subs.append(part[1:])\n        else:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"This should never happen.\")\n\n    # make a little exception when a name ends with digits, i.e. treat them\n    # as a subscript too.\n    m = _name_with_digits_p.match(name)\n    if m:\n        name, sub = m.groups()\n        subs.insert(0, sub)\n\n    return name, supers, subs\n\n\ndef requires_partial(expr):\n    \"\"\"Return whether a partial derivative symbol is required for printing\n\n    This requires checking how many free variables there are,\n    filtering out the ones that are integers. Per the MathML spec, `<mi>` is a token element that may only contain text. Browsers (Safari 12.0.2) don't render script elements nested inside `<mi>`, so `x2` disappeared entirely while plain `x` rendered fine.\n\n**Fix** (`sympy/printing/mathml.py`): The root element is now built directly:\n- No scripts → root is the `<mi>` element itself (`<mi>x</mi>`)\n- Subscript → root is `<msub>`\n- Superscript → root is `<msup>`\n- Both → root is `<msubsup>`\n\nThe `mathvariant=\"bold\"` attribute (for bold matrix symbols) is applied to whichever element is the root. This matches the fix that shipped in SymPy 1.4.\n\n**Result:** `x2` now prints as `<msub><mi>x</mi><mi>2</mi></msub>` — valid MathML that renders correctly.\n\n**Tests** (`sympy/printing/tests/test_mathml.py`): Updated `test_presentation_symbol` assertions to expect the script element as root.
